## 7. Institutional schema audit and profitability research

**Research proposals only; no admission policy, daemon, threshold or risk registry was changed. No profitability gain or replay certification claimed.**

### A. Actual schema and missing/suboptimal evidence

- Snapshot is sequential, not atomic: account/position/asset quotes differ by phase; each block needs event, receipt, availability timestamps, clockoffset uncertainty and maximumskew. Current Hyperdashblock11:00:22 precedes root11:00:24; unlike Cycle91 it is not future-relative to root. It still contains mixed historical dates; feature availability, not bar event-time alone, governs replay. Zero alerts do not establish no risk.
- BTC registeredR0 exists but telemetry initialR isnull. Join ticket/side/originalSL/initialpoints/cashR0/volume/sourcechecksum into the position block; preserve original values through manualmods/restarts/partialcloses. Missingrecords should emitUNKNOWN_INITIAL_RISK, never frozenATR. Current dispatcher fallback remains a proposed fix, not a runtime change here.
- L2 is aggregated20-level depth; walletL3 explicitly lacksorderIDs/fullqueuecoverage. Add exchange sequence ranges/checksums/gap recovery/tradeIDs and per-order events where available. Walletaddress is not proof ofEthereumvenue or directional institutional intent. Track actual recurrence, cancellation, refill and relocation instead of treating elapsedspan/demotion as continuity/pull.
- DJ/GER depth missing; raw `footprint_trapped_traders` price-by-time aggressor grid is absent, only derivedtrappedflag exists. Add buy/sellUSD perprice/window, diagonal/stackedimbalance, swept-extrema response, trappedinventory andpost-executionrefill. Missing data isunknown, notzero. Divergence labels are alerts, not absorption certificates.
- Publish actual TradFitick-windowstart/end, tickcap, coverage andunit; signedtickcounts are notUSD. Repeatedsession/15m/5m values may reflect overlapping1000-tick retention, not independentconfirmations. VolumeprofilesuseMT5tick/bar proxies, notexchangecontracts. Calendarweek/day boundaries and value-areaalgorithm need explicitprovenance; rolling~192bars are not necessarily calendarweek coverage.
- Coarsebins can make legal intraday stops geometrically expensive: ETH10points≈1.2035ATR/NAS100points≈2.7011ATR/SP10points≈2.1845ATR; FX0.01 bins≈20–25ATR. Publish unbinned triggerdensity, resolutionuncertainty and full candidate E/SL coverage. Deduplicate top/nearest aliases and stop/liquidationsemantics before aggregatefuel ratios. BTC stop remains hazardous even though one component is outside current truncated subsets; never silently zero or invent that component.

**Cycle92 priority addition:** publish persistence-cacheepoch/restartreason/previousstatechecksum, sourceevent sequences and maximumunobservedinterval. Archive122exactidentity recurrences reproducibly but do not silently rewrite currentmetadata or equate cache-reset with yank. Preserve useful priorreceipts across restart; distinguish recurrentpresence from uninterruptedorderlife. Unknown cause/coverage remains explicit. This is proposed, not implemented.

### B. Concrete mathematical feature additions

1. **Multi-level eventOFI:** perlevelk, `e_k = I(b_new>=b_old)*qbid_new − I(b_new<=b_old)*qbid_old − I(a_new<=a_old)*qask_new + I(a_new>=a_old)*qask_old`. Sumevents with predefinedlevelweights, normalise by reliable localdepth/activity, and separate executedtrades from cancellation/removal events. Current staticimbalance is not eventOFI; price-level alignment and venue must remain consistent.
2. **Microprice:** `mu=(ask*q_bid+bid*q_ask)/(q_bid+q_ask)`; publish`(mu−mid)/spread` pluscausal1/3/5m drift. Use samevenue/time/quantityunits and handleunknown/zerodenominators. Perpqueue information is not MT5OTCqueue priority.
3. **Aggressor termstructure:** `A_h=buy_usd/(buy_usd+sell_usd)` and`signed_fraction_h=(buy_usd−sell_usd)/(buy_usd+sell_usd)` forcomplete1/5/15/30m; publish slope/curvature,arrivalintensity,priceimpactperUSD andcoverage. BTCpositivewindows merit earlierWATCH alerts, not automaticearlierentry.
4. **Queue/wall decay:** cancellationhazard perunit at-risk exposure, execution/refill decomposition, refilllatency, relocationdistance andsurvival-to-touch withcensoredobservations. OrderIDs/events are required foractualqueue-ahead estimates; otherwise discloseaggregate-depthproxy. Do not label walletre-pegs asphysicallycontinuous without evidence.
5. **Absorption:** combine aggression against a heldlevel, failedpriceextension perunitnotional, decliningimpact, execution-followedreplenishment andsubsequentcompletedreclaim. Astatisticalscore requiresheld-out calibration; no arbitrarynewthreshold is adopted here. BTCreturningbid is not proof itswallet isunidirectional.
6. **Basis/volatilitysurface:** samevenueinstantaneousvsrollingbasis, residualquantiles, quoteage andjointspread/basis shocks. Storevolatility-byhorizon/side/depth-regime plusovershoot distributions; avoidindependentquantileaddition presented as guaranteedjointcoverage.
